\section{Problemas mixtos de aplicación}
\nivel{Nivel 1 - Básico}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textbf{\arabic*.}]
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 501.} Un número multiplicado por \(7\) y aumentado en \(5\) da \(75\). Hallar el número.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 502.} Un rectángulo tiene lados \(x+2\) y \(x+6\). Su área es \(96\). Hallar \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 503.} En una tienda, \(1\) cuadernos y \(5\) lápices cuestan \(40\) Bs; \(6\) cuadernos y \(2\) lápices cuestan \(44\) Bs. Hallar ambos precios.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 504.} Dos números suman \(12\) y su producto es \(20\). Hallar los números.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 505.} Un modelo se anula cuando \(P(x)=x^{3} - 11 x^{2} + 38 x - 40\). Hallar los valores de \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 506.} Un número multiplicado por \(7\) y aumentado en \(13\) da \(111\). Hallar el número.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 507.} Un rectángulo tiene lados \(x+5\) y \(x+6\). Su área es \(90\). Hallar \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 508.} En una tienda, \(4\) cuadernos y \(6\) lápices cuestan \(60\) Bs; \(3\) cuadernos y \(1\) lápices cuestan \(17\) Bs. Hallar ambos precios.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 509.} Dos números suman \(8\) y su producto es \(15\). Hallar los números.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 510.} Un modelo se anula cuando \(P(x)=2 x^{3} - 34 x^{2} + 188 x - 336\). Hallar los valores de \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 511.} Un número multiplicado por \(4\) y aumentado en \(2\) da \(50\). Hallar el número.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 512.} Un rectángulo tiene lados \(x+6\) y \(x+2\). Su área es \(77\). Hallar \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 513.} En una tienda, \(5\) cuadernos y \(3\) lápices cuestan \(58\) Bs; \(2\) cuadernos y \(6\) lápices cuestan \(76\) Bs. Hallar ambos precios.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 514.} Dos números suman \(17\) y su producto es \(70\). Hallar los números.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 515.} Un modelo se anula cuando \(P(x)=x^{3} - 18 x^{2} + 101 x - 168\). Hallar los valores de \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 516.} Un número multiplicado por \(6\) y aumentado en \(16\) da \(94\). Hallar el número.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 517.} Un rectángulo tiene lados \(x+5\) y \(x+2\). Su área es \(130\). Hallar \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 518.} En una tienda, \(2\) cuadernos y \(2\) lápices cuestan \(14\) Bs; \(3\) cuadernos y \(4\) lápices cuestan \(26\) Bs. Hallar ambos precios.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 519.} Dos números suman \(8\) y su producto es \(12\). Hallar los números.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 520.} Un modelo se anula cuando \(P(x)=x^{3} - 12 x^{2} + 41 x - 30\). Hallar los valores de \(x\).
\end{enumerate}
\end{multicols}

\item \textbf{Ejercicio 543.} En una tienda, \(7\) cuadernos y \(6\) lápices cuestan \(100\) Bs; \(1\) cuadernos y \(7\) lápices cuestan \(88\) Bs. Hallar ambos precios.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 544.} Dos números suman \(29\) y su producto es \(210\). Hallar los números.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 545.} Un modelo se anula cuando \(P(x)=3 x^{3} - 57 x^{2} + 330 x - 600\). Hallar los valores de \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 546.} Un número multiplicado por \(9\) y aumentado en \(6\) da \(186\). Hallar el número.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 547.} Un rectángulo tiene lados \(x+1\) y \(x+4\). Su área es \(54\). Hallar \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 548.} En una tienda, \(7\) cuadernos y \(3\) lápices cuestan \(36\) Bs; \(2\) cuadernos y \(8\) lápices cuestan \(46\) Bs. Hallar ambos precios.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 549.} Dos números suman \(17\) y su producto es \(42\). Hallar los números.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 550.} Un modelo se anula cuando \(P(x)=2 x^{3} - 22 x^{2} + 72 x - 72\). Hallar los valores de \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 551.} Un número multiplicado por \(8\) y aumentado en \(15\) da \(87\). Hallar el número.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 552.} Un rectángulo tiene lados \(x+5\) y \(x+8\). Su área es \(88\). Hallar \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 553.} En una tienda, \(1\) cuadernos y \(7\) lápices cuestan \(32\) Bs; \(2\) cuadernos y \(3\) lápices cuestan \(31\) Bs. Hallar ambos precios.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 554.} Dos números suman \(18\) y su producto es \(80\). Hallar los números.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 555.} Un modelo se anula cuando \(P(x)=3 x^{3} - 75 x^{2} + 618 x - 1680\). Hallar los valores de \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 556.} Un número multiplicado por \(7\) y aumentado en \(9\) da \(163\). Hallar el número.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 557.} Un rectángulo tiene lados \(x+7\) y \(x+3\). Su área es \(221\). Hallar \(x\).
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 558.} En una tienda, \(5\) cuadernos y \(1\) lápices cuestan \(50\) Bs; \(3\) cuadernos y \(8\) lápices cuestan \(67\) Bs. Hallar ambos precios.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 559.} Dos números suman \(16\) y su producto es \(39\). Hallar los números.
\item \textbf{Ejercicio 560.} Un modelo se anula cuando \(P(x)=2 x^{3} - 28 x^{2} + 98 x - 72\). Hallar los valores de \(x\).

\subsection{Ecuaciones cuadráticas mixtas}
\subsubsection*{Nivel 1 - Básico}\addcontentsline{toc}{subsubsection}{Ecuaciones cuadráticas mixtas - Nivel 1 - Básico}
\res{Ejercicio 101 - Factor común}
\[ 5 x^{2} - 25 x=0 \]
\[ 5x(x-5)=0 \]
\[ 5x=0 \quad \vee \quad x-5=0 \]
\[ x=0 \quad \vee \quad x=5 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{0,5\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 102 - Diferencia de cuadrados}
\[ 9 x^{2} - 225=0 \]
\[ (3x-15)(3x+15)=0 \]
\[ 3x-15=0 \quad \vee \quad 3x+15=0 \]
\[ x=5 \quad \vee \quad x=-5 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-5,5\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 103 - Aspas}
\[ x^{2} + 4 x - 21=0 \]
\[ (x-3)(x+7)=0 \]
\[ x=3 \quad \vee \quad x=-7 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-7,3\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 104 - Fórmula general}
\[ 3 x^{2} + 12 x - 1=0 \]
\[ a=3,\quad b=12,\quad c=-1 \]
\[ \Delta=b^2-4ac=(12)^2-4(3)(-1)=156 \]
\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{-12\pm\sqrt{156}}{6} \]
\[ \boxed{x_1=\frac{-12+\sqrt{156}}{6},\quad x_2=\frac{-12-\sqrt{156}}{6}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 105 - Factor común}
\[ 4 x^{2} - 20 x=0 \]
\[ 4x(x-5)=0 \]
\[ 4x=0 \quad \vee \quad x-5=0 \]
\[ x=0 \quad \vee \quad x=5 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{0,5\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 106 - Diferencia de cuadrados}
\[ x^{2} - 4=0 \]
\[ (1x-2)(1x+2)=0 \]
\[ 1x-2=0 \quad \vee \quad 1x+2=0 \]
\[ x=2 \quad \vee \quad x=-2 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-2,2\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 107 - Aspas}
\[ 2 x^{2} + 10 x + 8=0 \]
\[ (x+1)2(x+4)=0 \]
\[ x=-1 \quad \vee \quad x=-4 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-4,-1\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 108 - Fórmula general}
\[ 4 x^{2} + 12 x + 4=0 \]
\[ a=4,\quad b=12,\quad c=4 \]
\[ \Delta=b^2-4ac=(12)^2-4(4)(4)=80 \]
\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{-12\pm\sqrt{80}}{8} \]
\[ \boxed{x_1=\frac{-12+\sqrt{80}}{8},\quad x_2=\frac{-12-\sqrt{80}}{8}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 109 - Factor común}
\[ 4 x^{2} + 4 x=0 \]
\[ 4x(x+1)=0 \]
\[ 4x=0 \quad \vee \quad x+1=0 \]
\[ x=0 \quad \vee \quad x=-1 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{0,-1\}} \]

\[ 4 x^{2} - 36=0 \]

\[ 2x-6=0 \quad \vee \quad 2x+6=0 \]
\[ x=3 \quad \vee \quad x=-3 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-3,3\}} \]

\[ 5 x^{2} - 1=0 \]
\[ a=5,\quad b=0,\quad c=-1 \]

\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{0\pm\sqrt{20}}{10} \]

\[ 3 x^{2} - 15 x=0 \]

\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{-10\pm\sqrt{260}}{8} \]

\res{Ejercicio 120 - Fórmula general}

\[ a=4,\quad b=13,\quad c=-12 \]

\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{-13\pm\sqrt{361}}{8} \]

\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{15\pm\sqrt{45}}{10} \]

\[ 7 x^{2} - 14 x=0 \]
\[ 7x(x-2)=0 \]
\[ 7x=0 \quad \vee \quad x-2=0 \]
\[ x=0 \quad \vee \quad x=2 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{0,2\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 190 - Diferencia de cuadrados}
\[ 4 x^{2} - 16=0 \]
\[ (2x-4)(2x+4)=0 \]
\[ 2x-4=0 \quad \vee \quad 2x+4=0 \]
\[ x=2 \quad \vee \quad x=-2 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-2,2\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 191 - Aspas}
\[ 8 x^{2} + 16 x - 504=0 \]
\[ 4(x-7)2(x+9)=0 \]
\[ x=7 \quad \vee \quad x=-9 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-9,7\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 192 - Fórmula general}
\[ x^{2} - 4 x - 1=0 \]
\[ a=1,\quad b=-4,\quad c=-1 \]
\[ \Delta=b^2-4ac=(-4)^2-4(1)(-1)=20 \]
\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{4\pm\sqrt{20}}{2} \]
\[ \boxed{x_1=\frac{4+\sqrt{20}}{2},\quad x_2=\frac{4-\sqrt{20}}{2}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 193 - Factor común}
\[ 9 x^{2} + 9 x=0 \]
\[ 9x(x+1)=0 \]
\[ 9x=0 \quad \vee \quad x+1=0 \]
\[ x=0 \quad \vee \quad x=-1 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{0,-1\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 194 - Diferencia de cuadrados}
\[ 9 x^{2} - 225=0 \]
\[ (3x-15)(3x+15)=0 \]
\[ 3x-15=0 \quad \vee \quad 3x+15=0 \]
\[ x=5 \quad \vee \quad x=-5 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-5,5\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 195 - Aspas}
\[ 12 x^{2} - 48 x + 36=0 \]
\[ 6(x-1)2(x-3)=0 \]
\[ x=1 \quad \vee \quad x=3 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{1,3\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 196 - Fórmula general}
\[ 6 x^{2} + 20 x - 7=0 \]
\[ a=6,\quad b=20,\quad c=-7 \]
\[ \Delta=b^2-4ac=(20)^2-4(6)(-7)=568 \]
\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{-20\pm\sqrt{568}}{12} \]
\[ \boxed{x_1=\frac{-20+\sqrt{568}}{12},\quad x_2=\frac{-20-\sqrt{568}}{12}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 197 - Factor común}
\[ 9 x^{2} + 36 x=0 \]
\[ 9x(x+4)=0 \]
\[ 9x=0 \quad \vee \quad x+4=0 \]
\[ x=0 \quad \vee \quad x=-4 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{0,-4\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 198 - Diferencia de cuadrados}
\[ x^{2} - 36=0 \]
\[ (1x-6)(1x+6)=0 \]
\[ 1x-6=0 \quad \vee \quad 1x+6=0 \]
\[ x=6 \quad \vee \quad x=-6 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-6,6\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 199 - Aspas}
\[ 10 x^{2} + 20 x - 30=0 \]
\[ 5(x+3)2(x-1)=0 \]
\[ x=-3 \quad \vee \quad x=1 \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{-3,1\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 200 - Fórmula general}
\[ 8 x^{2} - 19 x + 7=0 \]
\[ a=8,\quad b=-19,\quad c=7 \]
\[ \Delta=b^2-4ac=(-19)^2-4(8)(7)=137 \]
\[ x=\frac{-b\pm\sqrt{\Delta}}{2a}=\frac{19\pm\sqrt{137}}{16} \]
\[ \boxed{x_1=\frac{19+\sqrt{137}}{16},\quad x_2=\frac{19-\sqrt{137}}{16}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}

\[ P(x)=(x-1)(x-4)(x-5) \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{1,4,5\}} \]

\subsection{Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\subsubsection*{Nivel 1 - Básico}\addcontentsline{toc}{subsubsection}{Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales - Nivel 1 - Básico}
\res{Ejercicio 301 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 4 x - 5 y=16\\x + 3 y=-4\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-4&-5\\1&3\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(3)-(1)(-5)=-7 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}16&-5\\-4&3\end{vmatrix}=(16)(3)-(-4)(-5)=28 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-4&16\\1&-4\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(-4)-(1)(16)=0 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{28}{-7}=-4,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{0}{-7}=0 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-4,0)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 302 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 5 x + y=33\\3 x + 3 y=-9\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-5&1\\3&3\end{vmatrix}=(-5)(3)-(3)(1)=-18 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}33&1\\-9&3\end{vmatrix}=(33)(3)-(-9)(1)=108 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-5&33\\3&-9\end{vmatrix}=(-5)(-9)-(3)(33)=-54 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{108}{-18}=-6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-54}{-18}=3 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-6,3)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 303 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}4 x + 2 y=10\\- 3 x + 5 y=12\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}4&2\\-3&5\end{vmatrix}=(4)(5)-(-3)(2)=26 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}10&2\\12&5\end{vmatrix}=(10)(5)-(12)(2)=26 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}4&10\\-3&12\end{vmatrix}=(4)(12)-(-3)(10)=78 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{26}{26}=1,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{78}{26}=3 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(1,3)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 304 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}3 x + y=-25\\- x + 2 y=-1\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}3&1\\-1&2\end{vmatrix}=(3)(2)-(-1)(1)=7 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-25&1\\-1&2\end{vmatrix}=(-25)(2)-(-1)(1)=-49 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}3&-25\\-1&-1\end{vmatrix}=(3)(-1)-(-1)(-25)=-28 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-49}{7}=-7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-28}{7}=-4 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-7,-4)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 305 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}3 x - 5 y=18\\- x - 5 y=14\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}3&-5\\-1&-5\end{vmatrix}=(3)(-5)-(-1)(-5)=-20 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}18&-5\\14&-5\end{vmatrix}=(18)(-5)-(14)(-5)=-20 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}3&18\\-1&14\end{vmatrix}=(3)(14)-(-1)(18)=60 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-20}{-20}=1,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{60}{-20}=-3 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(1,-3)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 306 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- x + 4 y=12\\3 x - 5 y=-8\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-1&4\\3&-5\end{vmatrix}=(-1)(-5)-(3)(4)=-7 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}12&4\\-8&-5\end{vmatrix}=(12)(-5)-(-8)(4)=-28 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-1&12\\3&-8\end{vmatrix}=(-1)(-8)-(3)(12)=-28 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-28}{-7}=4,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-28}{-7}=4 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(4,4)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 307 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 2 x - 3 y=21\\x - y=2\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-2&-3\\1&-1\end{vmatrix}=(-2)(-1)-(1)(-3)=5 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}21&-3\\2&-1\end{vmatrix}=(21)(-1)-(2)(-3)=-15 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-2&21\\1&2\end{vmatrix}=(-2)(2)-(1)(21)=-25 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-15}{5}=-3,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-25}{5}=-5 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-3,-5)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 308 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}5 x - 2 y=11\\- 2 x - 3 y=7\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}5&-2\\-2&-3\end{vmatrix}=(5)(-3)-(-2)(-2)=-19 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}11&-2\\7&-3\end{vmatrix}=(11)(-3)-(7)(-2)=-19 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}5&11\\-2&7\end{vmatrix}=(5)(7)-(-2)(11)=57 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-19}{-19}=1,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{57}{-19}=-3 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(1,-3)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 309 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}2 x - 4 y=-28\\2 x + 4 y=4\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}2&-4\\2&4\end{vmatrix}=(2)(4)-(2)(-4)=16 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-28&-4\\4&4\end{vmatrix}=(-28)(4)-(4)(-4)=-96 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}2&-28\\2&4\end{vmatrix}=(2)(4)-(2)(-28)=64 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-96}{16}=-6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{64}{16}=4 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-6,4)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 310 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}x + y=8\\- 4 x - 5 y=-37\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}1&1\\-4&-5\end{vmatrix}=(1)(-5)-(-4)(1)=-1 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}8&1\\-37&-5\end{vmatrix}=(8)(-5)-(-37)(1)=-3 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}1&8\\-4&-37\end{vmatrix}=(1)(-37)-(-4)(8)=-5 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-3}{-1}=3,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-5}{-1}=5 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(3,5)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 311 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}4 x - 2 y=24\\4 x + y=30\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}4&-2\\4&1\end{vmatrix}=(4)(1)-(4)(-2)=12 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}24&-2\\30&1\end{vmatrix}=(24)(1)-(30)(-2)=84 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}4&24\\4&30\end{vmatrix}=(4)(30)-(4)(24)=24 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{84}{12}=7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{24}{12}=2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(7,2)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 312 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 3 x + 3 y=-27\\- 2 x - 5 y=24\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-3&3\\-2&-5\end{vmatrix}=(-3)(-5)-(-2)(3)=21 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-27&3\\24&-5\end{vmatrix}=(-27)(-5)-(24)(3)=63 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-3&-27\\-2&24\end{vmatrix}=(-3)(24)-(-2)(-27)=-126 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{63}{21}=3,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-126}{21}=-6 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(3,-6)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 313 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}

\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-16&4\\-20&-4\end{vmatrix}=(-16)(-4)-(-20)(4)=144 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}2&-16\\4&-20\end{vmatrix}=(2)(-20)-(4)(-16)=24 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{144}{-24}=-6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{24}{-24}=-1 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-6,-1)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}

\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}5&25\\-3&9\end{vmatrix}=(5)(9)-(-3)(25)=120 \]

\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(2,5)} \]

\res{Ejercicio 343 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 4 x - 7 y=13\\- 9 x - 8 y=6\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-4&-7\\-9&-8\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(-8)-(-9)(-7)=-31 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}13&-7\\6&-8\end{vmatrix}=(13)(-8)-(6)(-7)=-62 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-4&13\\-9&6\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(6)-(-9)(13)=93 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-62}{-31}=2,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{93}{-31}=-3 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(2,-3)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 344 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}x + 8 y=-17\\6 x + 6 y=24\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}1&8\\6&6\end{vmatrix}=(1)(6)-(6)(8)=-42 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-17&8\\24&6\end{vmatrix}=(-17)(6)-(24)(8)=-294 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}1&-17\\6&24\end{vmatrix}=(1)(24)-(6)(-17)=126 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-294}{-42}=7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{126}{-42}=-3 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(7,-3)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 345 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}3 x + 9 y=-69\\4 x + 7 y=-62\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}3&9\\4&7\end{vmatrix}=(3)(7)-(4)(9)=-15 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-69&9\\-62&7\end{vmatrix}=(-69)(7)-(-62)(9)=75 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}3&-69\\4&-62\end{vmatrix}=(3)(-62)-(4)(-69)=90 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{75}{-15}=-5,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{90}{-15}=-6 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-5,-6)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 346 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- x + y=10\\2 x + 5 y=43\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-1&1\\2&5\end{vmatrix}=(-1)(5)-(2)(1)=-7 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}10&1\\43&5\end{vmatrix}=(10)(5)-(43)(1)=7 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-1&10\\2&43\end{vmatrix}=(-1)(43)-(2)(10)=-63 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{7}{-7}=-1,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-63}{-7}=9 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-1,9)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 347 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 5 x - 7 y=33\\8 x - 4 y=84\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-5&-7\\8&-4\end{vmatrix}=(-5)(-4)-(8)(-7)=76 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}33&-7\\84&-4\end{vmatrix}=(33)(-4)-(84)(-7)=456 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-5&33\\8&84\end{vmatrix}=(-5)(84)-(8)(33)=-684 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{456}{76}=6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-684}{76}=-9 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(6,-9)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 348 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}6 x - 4 y=50\\4 x + 7 y=-44\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}6&-4\\4&7\end{vmatrix}=(6)(7)-(4)(-4)=58 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}50&-4\\-44&7\end{vmatrix}=(50)(7)-(-44)(-4)=174 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}6&50\\4&-44\end{vmatrix}=(6)(-44)-(4)(50)=-464 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{174}{58}=3,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-464}{58}=-8 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(3,-8)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 349 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 4 x + 8 y=24\\9 x - y=-71\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-4&8\\9&-1\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(-1)-(9)(8)=-68 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}24&8\\-71&-1\end{vmatrix}=(24)(-1)-(-71)(8)=544 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-4&24\\9&-71\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(-71)-(9)(24)=68 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{544}{-68}=-8,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{68}{-68}=-1 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-8,-1)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 350 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 3 x + 7 y=4\\- 6 x + 5 y=26\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-3&7\\-6&5\end{vmatrix}=(-3)(5)-(-6)(7)=27 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}4&7\\26&5\end{vmatrix}=(4)(5)-(26)(7)=-162 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-3&4\\-6&26\end{vmatrix}=(-3)(26)-(-6)(4)=-54 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-162}{27}=-6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-54}{27}=-2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-6,-2)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 351 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}4 x - 8 y=-20\\5 x - 4 y=-37\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}4&-8\\5&-4\end{vmatrix}=(4)(-4)-(5)(-8)=24 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-20&-8\\-37&-4\end{vmatrix}=(-20)(-4)-(-37)(-8)=-216 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}4&-20\\5&-37\end{vmatrix}=(4)(-37)-(5)(-20)=-48 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-216}{24}=-9,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-48}{24}=-2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-9,-2)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 352 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}7 x - 9 y=51\\- 6 x + 2 y=-38\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}7&-9\\-6&2\end{vmatrix}=(7)(2)-(-6)(-9)=-40 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}51&-9\\-38&2\end{vmatrix}=(51)(2)-(-38)(-9)=-240 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}7&51\\-6&-38\end{vmatrix}=(7)(-38)-(-6)(51)=40 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-240}{-40}=6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{40}{-40}=-1 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(6,-1)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 353 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 7 x + y=10\\5 x - y=-8\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-7&1\\5&-1\end{vmatrix}=(-7)(-1)-(5)(1)=2 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}10&1\\-8&-1\end{vmatrix}=(10)(-1)-(-8)(1)=-2 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-7&10\\5&-8\end{vmatrix}=(-7)(-8)-(5)(10)=6 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-2}{2}=-1,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{6}{2}=3 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-1,3)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 354 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}5 x - 4 y=36\\7 x - 8 y=48\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}5&-4\\7&-8\end{vmatrix}=(5)(-8)-(7)(-4)=-12 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}36&-4\\48&-8\end{vmatrix}=(36)(-8)-(48)(-4)=-96 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}5&36\\7&48\end{vmatrix}=(5)(48)-(7)(36)=-12 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-96}{-12}=8,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-12}{-12}=1 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(8,1)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 355 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}

\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-4&-4\\3&1\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(1)-(3)(-4)=8 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-4&-4\\-1&1\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(1)-(-1)(-4)=-8 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-4&-4\\3&-1\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(-1)-(3)(-4)=16 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-8}{8}=-1,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{16}{8}=2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-1,2)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 356 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}8 x + 6 y=-12\\- 4 x - 7 y=-2\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}8&6\\-4&-7\end{vmatrix}=(8)(-7)-(-4)(6)=-32 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-12&6\\-2&-7\end{vmatrix}=(-12)(-7)-(-2)(6)=96 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}8&-12\\-4&-2\end{vmatrix}=(8)(-2)-(-4)(-12)=-64 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{96}{-32}=-3,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-64}{-32}=2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-3,2)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 357 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}4 x - 2 y=-16\\9 x - y=-57\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}4&-2\\9&-1\end{vmatrix}=(4)(-1)-(9)(-2)=14 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-16&-2\\-57&-1\end{vmatrix}=(-16)(-1)-(-57)(-2)=-98 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}4&-16\\9&-57\end{vmatrix}=(4)(-57)-(9)(-16)=-84 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-98}{14}=-7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-84}{14}=-6 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-7,-6)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 358 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 9 x - 8 y=-9\\3 x - 8 y=3\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-9&-8\\3&-8\end{vmatrix}=(-9)(-8)-(3)(-8)=96 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-9&-8\\3&-8\end{vmatrix}=(-9)(-8)-(3)(-8)=96 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-9&-9\\3&3\end{vmatrix}=(-9)(3)-(3)(-9)=0 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{96}{96}=1,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{0}{96}=0 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(1,0)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 359 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}2 x + 3 y=13\\2 x - 2 y=18\end{cases} \]

\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}13&3\\18&-2\end{vmatrix}=(13)(-2)-(18)(3)=-80 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}2&13\\2&18\end{vmatrix}=(2)(18)-(2)(13)=10 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-80}{-10}=8,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{10}{-10}=-1 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(8,-1)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 360 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}8 x + 2 y=52\\- 4 x - 6 y=-16\end{cases} \]

\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}52&2\\-16&-6\end{vmatrix}=(52)(-6)-(-16)(2)=-280 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}8&52\\-4&-16\end{vmatrix}=(8)(-16)-(-4)(52)=80 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-280}{-40}=7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{80}{-40}=-2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(7,-2)} \]

\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-103&-8\\41&1\end{vmatrix}=(-103)(1)-(41)(-8)=225 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-7&-103\\4&41\end{vmatrix}=(-7)(41)-(4)(-103)=125 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{225}{25}=9,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{125}{25}=5 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(9,5)} \]

\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{36}{-4}=-9,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-24}{-4}=6 \]

\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}2&-37\\-5&79\end{vmatrix}=(2)(79)-(-5)(-37)=-27 \]

\res{Ejercicio 376 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 8 x + 7 y=76\\- 6 x + 9 y=72\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-8&7\\-6&9\end{vmatrix}=(-8)(9)-(-6)(7)=-30 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}76&7\\72&9\end{vmatrix}=(76)(9)-(72)(7)=180 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-8&76\\-6&72\end{vmatrix}=(-8)(72)-(-6)(76)=-120 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{180}{-30}=-6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-120}{-30}=4 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-6,4)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 377 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- x + 4 y=-46\\- 5 x - 11 y=49\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-1&4\\-5&-11\end{vmatrix}=(-1)(-11)-(-5)(4)=31 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-46&4\\49&-11\end{vmatrix}=(-46)(-11)-(49)(4)=310 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-1&-46\\-5&49\end{vmatrix}=(-1)(49)-(-5)(-46)=-279 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{310}{31}=10,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-279}{31}=-9 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(10,-9)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 378 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}5 x - 7 y=-88\\- 2 x - 3 y=-17\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}5&-7\\-2&-3\end{vmatrix}=(5)(-3)-(-2)(-7)=-29 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-88&-7\\-17&-3\end{vmatrix}=(-88)(-3)-(-17)(-7)=145 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}5&-88\\-2&-17\end{vmatrix}=(5)(-17)-(-2)(-88)=-261 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{145}{-29}=-5,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-261}{-29}=9 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-5,9)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 379 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}x + 8 y=36\\9 x - 11 y=-91\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}1&8\\9&-11\end{vmatrix}=(1)(-11)-(9)(8)=-83 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}36&8\\-91&-11\end{vmatrix}=(36)(-11)-(-91)(8)=332 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}1&36\\9&-91\end{vmatrix}=(1)(-91)-(9)(36)=-415 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{332}{-83}=-4,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-415}{-83}=5 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-4,5)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 380 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}4 x + 8 y=28\\- 6 x + 5 y=-8\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}4&8\\-6&5\end{vmatrix}=(4)(5)-(-6)(8)=68 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}28&8\\-8&5\end{vmatrix}=(28)(5)-(-8)(8)=204 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}4&28\\-6&-8\end{vmatrix}=(4)(-8)-(-6)(28)=136 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{204}{68}=3,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{136}{68}=2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(3,2)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}

\res{Ejercicio 381 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 12 x - 3 y=-15\\9 x - y=47\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-12&-3\\9&-1\end{vmatrix}=(-12)(-1)-(9)(-3)=39 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-15&-3\\47&-1\end{vmatrix}=(-15)(-1)-(47)(-3)=156 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-12&-15\\9&47\end{vmatrix}=(-12)(47)-(9)(-15)=-429 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{156}{39}=4,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-429}{39}=-11 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(4,-11)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 382 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}2 x - 12 y=146\\7 x + 10 y=-61\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}2&-12\\7&10\end{vmatrix}=(2)(10)-(7)(-12)=104 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}146&-12\\-61&10\end{vmatrix}=(146)(10)-(-61)(-12)=728 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}2&146\\7&-61\end{vmatrix}=(2)(-61)-(7)(146)=-1144 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{728}{104}=7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-1144}{104}=-11 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(7,-11)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 383 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 4 x + 2 y=-40\\12 x + 6 y=0\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-4&2\\12&6\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(6)-(12)(2)=-48 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-40&2\\0&6\end{vmatrix}=(-40)(6)-(0)(2)=-240 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-4&-40\\12&0\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(0)-(12)(-40)=480 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-240}{-48}=5,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{480}{-48}=-10 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(5,-10)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 384 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}5 x - 8 y=94\\- 4 x + 12 y=-120\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}5&-8\\-4&12\end{vmatrix}=(5)(12)-(-4)(-8)=28 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}94&-8\\-120&12\end{vmatrix}=(94)(12)-(-120)(-8)=168 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}5&94\\-4&-120\end{vmatrix}=(5)(-120)-(-4)(94)=-224 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{168}{28}=6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-224}{28}=-8 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(6,-8)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 385 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}8 x - 6 y=-48\\- 9 x - 11 y=54\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}8&-6\\-9&-11\end{vmatrix}=(8)(-11)-(-9)(-6)=-142 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-48&-6\\54&-11\end{vmatrix}=(-48)(-11)-(54)(-6)=852 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}8&-48\\-9&54\end{vmatrix}=(8)(54)-(-9)(-48)=0 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{852}{-142}=-6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{0}{-142}=0 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-6,0)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 386 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 11 x + y=4\\x - 8 y=-32\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-11&1\\1&-8\end{vmatrix}=(-11)(-8)-(1)(1)=87 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}4&1\\-32&-8\end{vmatrix}=(4)(-8)-(-32)(1)=0 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-11&4\\1&-32\end{vmatrix}=(-11)(-32)-(1)(4)=348 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{0}{87}=0,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{348}{87}=4 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(0,4)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 387 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}3 x + 8 y=-22\\- 7 x + 12 y=-10\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}3&8\\-7&12\end{vmatrix}=(3)(12)-(-7)(8)=92 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-22&8\\-10&12\end{vmatrix}=(-22)(12)-(-10)(8)=-184 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}3&-22\\-7&-10\end{vmatrix}=(3)(-10)-(-7)(-22)=-184 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-184}{92}=-2,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-184}{92}=-2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-2,-2)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 388 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}5 x + 11 y=-4\\- 12 x - 13 y=-44\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}5&11\\-12&-13\end{vmatrix}=(5)(-13)-(-12)(11)=67 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-4&11\\-44&-13\end{vmatrix}=(-4)(-13)-(-44)(11)=536 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}5&-4\\-12&-44\end{vmatrix}=(5)(-44)-(-12)(-4)=-268 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{536}{67}=8,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-268}{67}=-4 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(8,-4)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 389 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 12 x + 13 y=59\\2 x + 3 y=47\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-12&13\\2&3\end{vmatrix}=(-12)(3)-(2)(13)=-62 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}59&13\\47&3\end{vmatrix}=(59)(3)-(47)(13)=-434 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-12&59\\2&47\end{vmatrix}=(-12)(47)-(2)(59)=-682 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-434}{-62}=7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-682}{-62}=11 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(7,11)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 390 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}3 x - 2 y=6\\- 7 x - 6 y=-78\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}3&-2\\-7&-6\end{vmatrix}=(3)(-6)-(-7)(-2)=-32 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}6&-2\\-78&-6\end{vmatrix}=(6)(-6)-(-78)(-2)=-192 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}3&6\\-7&-78\end{vmatrix}=(3)(-78)-(-7)(6)=-192 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-192}{-32}=6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-192}{-32}=6 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(6,6)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 391 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}12 x + 9 y=168\\- 5 x + 10 y=40\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}12&9\\-5&10\end{vmatrix}=(12)(10)-(-5)(9)=165 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}168&9\\40&10\end{vmatrix}=(168)(10)-(40)(9)=1320 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}12&168\\-5&40\end{vmatrix}=(12)(40)-(-5)(168)=1320 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{1320}{165}=8,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{1320}{165}=8 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(8,8)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 392 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 10 x - 13 y=-73\\x + 11 y=17\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-10&-13\\1&11\end{vmatrix}=(-10)(11)-(1)(-13)=-97 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-73&-13\\17&11\end{vmatrix}=(-73)(11)-(17)(-13)=-582 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-10&-73\\1&17\end{vmatrix}=(-10)(17)-(1)(-73)=-97 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-582}{-97}=6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-97}{-97}=1 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(6,1)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 393 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}6 x - 10 y=18\\- 13 x + 2 y=79\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}6&-10\\-13&2\end{vmatrix}=(6)(2)-(-13)(-10)=-118 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}18&-10\\79&2\end{vmatrix}=(18)(2)-(79)(-10)=826 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}6&18\\-13&79\end{vmatrix}=(6)(79)-(-13)(18)=708 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{826}{-118}=-7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{708}{-118}=-6 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-7,-6)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 394 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 11 x - 9 y=-86\\- 10 x - 3 y=-73\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-11&-9\\-10&-3\end{vmatrix}=(-11)(-3)-(-10)(-9)=-57 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-86&-9\\-73&-3\end{vmatrix}=(-86)(-3)-(-73)(-9)=-399 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-11&-86\\-10&-73\end{vmatrix}=(-11)(-73)-(-10)(-86)=-57 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-399}{-57}=7,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-57}{-57}=1 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(7,1)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 395 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}2 x - 9 y=-69\\12 x + y=-29\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}2&-9\\12&1\end{vmatrix}=(2)(1)-(12)(-9)=110 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-69&-9\\-29&1\end{vmatrix}=(-69)(1)-(-29)(-9)=-330 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}2&-69\\12&-29\end{vmatrix}=(2)(-29)-(12)(-69)=770 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-330}{110}=-3,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{770}{110}=7 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-3,7)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 396 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}5 x + 4 y=34\\8 x + 8 y=64\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}5&4\\8&8\end{vmatrix}=(5)(8)-(8)(4)=8 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}34&4\\64&8\end{vmatrix}=(34)(8)-(64)(4)=16 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}5&34\\8&64\end{vmatrix}=(5)(64)-(8)(34)=48 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{16}{8}=2,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{48}{8}=6 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(2,6)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 397 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}10 x - y=-50\\3 x + 9 y=-108\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}10&-1\\3&9\end{vmatrix}=(10)(9)-(3)(-1)=93 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}-50&-1\\-108&9\end{vmatrix}=(-50)(9)-(-108)(-1)=-558 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}10&-50\\3&-108\end{vmatrix}=(10)(-108)-(3)(-50)=-930 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-558}{93}=-6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{-930}{93}=-10 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-6,-10)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 398 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 8 x - y=33\\10 x + 12 y=-52\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-8&-1\\10&12\end{vmatrix}=(-8)(12)-(10)(-1)=-86 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}33&-1\\-52&12\end{vmatrix}=(33)(12)-(-52)(-1)=344 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-8&33\\10&-52\end{vmatrix}=(-8)(-52)-(10)(33)=86 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{344}{-86}=-4,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{86}{-86}=-1 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-4,-1)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 399 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 13 x + 5 y=107\\- 9 x + 12 y=57\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-13&5\\-9&12\end{vmatrix}=(-13)(12)-(-9)(5)=-111 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}107&5\\57&12\end{vmatrix}=(107)(12)-(57)(5)=999 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-13&107\\-9&57\end{vmatrix}=(-13)(57)-(-9)(107)=222 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{999}{-111}=-9,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{222}{-111}=-2 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-9,-2)} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 400 - Sistemas de ecuaciones lineales}
\[ \begin{cases}- 5 x + y=41\\- 7 x - 10 y=-68\end{cases} \]
\[ D=\begin{vmatrix}-5&1\\-7&-10\end{vmatrix}=(-5)(-10)-(-7)(1)=57 \]
\[ D_x=\begin{vmatrix}41&1\\-68&-10\end{vmatrix}=(41)(-10)-(-68)(1)=-342 \]
\[ D_y=\begin{vmatrix}-5&41\\-7&-68\end{vmatrix}=(-5)(-68)-(-7)(41)=627 \]
\[ x=\frac{D_x}{D}=\frac{-342}{57}=-6,\qquad y=\frac{D_y}{D}=\frac{627}{57}=11 \]
\[ \boxed{(x,y)=(-6,11)} \]

\[ x=\frac{-140}{-28}=5,\qquad y=\frac{-196}{-28}=7 \]

\[\begin{array}{r|rrrr}4 & 1 & -11 & 38 & -40\\  &  & 4 & -28 & 40\\\hline  & 1 & -7 & 10 & 0\end{array}\]

\[ \boxed{x\in\{2,4,5\}} \]

\[ P(x)=(x-1)(x^{2} - 19 x + 84) \]
\[ P(x)=(x-1)(x-12)(x-7) \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{1,7,12\}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 591 - Problema}
\[ x=\text{número} \]
\[ 9x+20=110 \]
\[ 9x=90 \]
\[ \boxed{x=10} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 592 - Problema}
\[ (x+1)(x+7)=112 \]
\[ x^{2} + 8 x - 105=0 \]
\[ (x-7)(x+15)=0 \]
\[ x=7\quad \vee \quad x=-15 \]
\[ \boxed{x=7} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 593 - Problema}
\[ x=\text{cuaderno},\qquad y=\text{lápiz} \]
\[ \begin{cases}7x+10y=225\\6x+2y=114\end{cases} \]
\[ D=-46,\quad D_x=-690,\quad D_y=-552 \]
\[ x=\frac{-690}{-46}=15,\qquad y=\frac{-552}{-46}=12 \]
\[ \boxed{x=15\text{ Bs},\quad y=12\text{ Bs}} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}
\res{Ejercicio 594 - Problema}
\[ x+y=25,\qquad xy=156 \]
\[ t^2-25t+156=0 \]
\[ (t-12)(t-13)=0 \]
\[ \boxed{12\text{ y }13} \]
\vspace{1mm}\hrule\vspace{2mm}

\[ P(x)=(x-3)(x^{2} - 17 x + 66) \]
\[ P(x)=(x-3)(x-6)(x-11) \]
\[ \boxed{x\in\{3,6,11\}} \]
